### [90] [Projection-dependent VGluT1 and VGluT2 expression and terminal morphology in mouse visual circuits](https://www.biorxiv.org/content/10.64898/2026.08.31.748331v1)
*Laliberte, G.,Tremblay-Laliberte, R.,Boire, D.*

Main category: bioRxiv.Neuroscience

TL;DR: 本研究结合顺行示踪与VGluT1/VGluT2免疫荧光双重标记，系统分析了成年小鼠视觉系统中多条通路（初级/次级视皮层、外侧膝状体、外侧后丘脑核和上丘）的谷氨酸能突触末梢表型，发现投射类型与VGluT亚型之间存在关联，但投射方向或驱动-调节功能并不能完全由转运体种类或末梢大小单一指标预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VGluT1和VGluT2分布互补，被提出分别标记下行调节性通路和上行驱动样通路，但这种对应关系很少在解剖学确认的投射中被直接检验，作者希望系统验证这一假说在小鼠视觉系统中的适用性。

Method: 采用顺行Phaseolus vulgaris leucoagglutinin（PHA-L）示踪，结合VGluT1和VGluT2双标免疫荧光，在成年小鼠中鉴定来自初级与次级视皮层、外侧膝状体（LGN）、外侧后丘脑核（LP）和上丘（SC）的谷氨酸能突触末梢，并对其VGluT亚型、共定位及形态学参数进行定量分析。

Result: 在2,178个单标记突触末梢中，皮质丘脑、皮质脑桥、皮质顶盖、皮质纹状体和皮质-皮质反馈投射以VGluT1为主；而丘脑皮质、顶盖丘脑、丘脑纹状体及顶盖脑桥投射几乎全部为VGluT2阳性。投射方向不能完全预测转运体表型：上行皮质-皮质投射仍以VGluT1为主，而下行顶盖脑桥投射却完全为VGluT2阳性。2,190个VGluT免疫阳性末梢中仅12个检测到VGluT1/VGluT2共定位。形态学分析显示VGluT亚型与投射方向存在交互作用：上行投射中VGluT2阳性末梢较大，下行投射中无亚型差异。

Conclusion: VGluT表型与视觉系统投射类别存在关联，两种转运体可作为通路组织的有效标记物，但单独的转运体身份或末梢大小均不能作为投射方向或驱动-调节功能的不变分子指标。

Abstract: Vesicular glutamate transporters 1 and 2 (VGluT1 and VGluT2) exhibit largely complementary distributions and have been proposed as molecular markers of descending modulatory and ascending driver-like pathways, respectively. However, this correspondence has rarely been tested directly in anatomically identified projections. We combined anterograde Phaseolus vulgaris leucoagglutinin tracing with simultaneous VGluT1 and VGluT2 immunofluorescence to characterize glutamatergic boutons arising from the primary and secondary visual cortices, lateral geniculate nucleus, lateral posterior thalamic nucleus, and superior colliculus in adult mice. Among 2,178 singly labelled boutons, VGluT1 predominated in corticothalamic, corticopontine, corticotectal, corticostriatal, and corticocortical feedback projections. Conversely, thalamocortical, tectothalamic, thalamostriatal, and tectopontine projections were almost exclusively VGluT2-positive. Projection direction did not fully predict transporter phenotype: ascending corticocortical projections remained predominantly VGluT1-positive, whereas the descending tectopontine projection was exclusively VGluT2-positive. Only 12 of 2,190 VGluT-immunoreactive boutons exhibited detectable VGluT1/VGluT2 colocalization. Morphometric analyses further revealed an interaction between VGluT isoform and projection direction. Within ascending projections, VGluT2-positive boutons and puncta were larger than their VGluT1-positive counterparts, whereas no isoform-related difference in bouton area was detected within descending projections. These findings demonstrate an association between VGluT phenotype and projection class in the mouse visual system. VGluT isoforms therefore provide informative markers of pathway organization, but neither transporter identity nor terminal size alone constitutes an invariant molecular indicator of projection direction or driver-modulator function.

</details>


### [91] [Posterior parietal cortex guides sensorimotor associative learning by linking sensation to distal action](https://www.biorxiv.org/content/10.64898/2026.04.19.719425v2)
*Zhu, Q.,Wang, Y.,Huang, Y.,Zhang, J.,Yang, X.,Li, A.,Li, S.,Guo, Z. V.*

Main category: bioRxiv.Neuroscience

TL;DR: 本文通过皮层-wide单细胞分辨率钙成像和光遗传学实验，发现rostro-lateral后顶叶皮层(PPC-rl)在学习期间通过持续性延迟期活动，增强低维通信子空间来对齐感觉运动协同波动，从而跨越时间延迟桥接触觉刺激与延迟舔舐行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 感觉运动联想学习的挑战在于当感觉线索与运动反应之间存在时间延迟时，二者缺乏共活性，限制了经典的Hebbian可塑性机制。理解大脑如何在延迟条件下将感觉信息与远端动作关联起来，尚缺乏清晰的皮质环路机制。

Method: 1) 采用皮层-wide钙成像技术，单细胞分辨率同时追踪约16,000个神经元，覆盖感觉、运动及联络皮质；2) 识别PPC-rl在学习早期呈现持续延迟期活动，且随专业技能掌握而衰减；3) 光遗传学沉默实验验证PPC-rl的功能必要性；4) 分析学习过程中跨区域感觉运动耦合增强及低维通信子空间的变化；5) 构建生物合理的网络模型，模拟PPC-rl持续性、子空间对齐与奖励门控Hebbian塑性的协同作用。

Result: 研究发现PPC-rl是连接触觉刺激与延迟舔舐反应的关键皮质中枢。光遗传学沉默PPC-rl会减慢学习速度但不损害熟练运动执行。学习增强了区域内和区域间的感觉运动耦合，PPC-rl通过放大低维通信子空间实现感觉运动协同波动的对齐。网络模型验证了PPC-rl的持续性活动、子空间对齐及奖励门控Hebbian塑性三者协同支持延迟联想学习。

Conclusion: 本研究表明PPC-rl作为皮质枢纽，在学习过程中通过维持时间连续性将感觉信息与延迟运动动作联系起来。该发现揭示了延迟联想学习中跨越时间间隔的皮质环路机制，为理解时间延迟条件下的感觉运动整合提供了新的神经基础。

Abstract: Sensorimotor associative learning is challenged when sensory cues and motor responses are separated by a delay, limiting the coactivity required for Hebbian plasticity. Here we identify the rostro-lateral posterior parietal cortex (PPC-rl) as a cortical hub that bridges tactile stimuli and delayed licking during learning. Using cortex-wide calcium imaging with single-cell resolution to track ~16,000 neurons simultaneously across sensory, motor, and association cortices, we find that PPC-rl exhibits sustained delay-epoch activity early in learning that diminishes with expertise. Optogenetic silencing slows learning without impairing expert motor execution. Learning strengthens intra- and inter-areal sensorimotor coupling, and PPC-rl mediates this process by amplifying a low-dimensional communication subspace that aligns sensorimotor co-fluctuations. A biologically plausible network model shows that PPC-rl persistence, subspace alignment, and reward-gated Hebbian plasticity synergize to support delayed association. These findings uncover a PPC-rl based cortical mechanism that maintains temporal continuity to link sensation with distal action during learning.

</details>


### [92] [tACS increases alpha burst duration in a frequency- and montage-specific manner](https://www.biorxiv.org/content/10.64898/2026.08.31.747228v1)
*Rodriguez Larios, J.,Ragone, A.,Chauhan, S.,Koizumi, K.,Di Bernardi Luft, C.*

Main category: bioRxiv.Neuroscience

TL;DR: 本研究通过2x2设计（后部/额顶叶位置 × 10Hz/6Hz频率）加无刺激对照组，在158名健康被试的持续注意任务中探讨tACS对神经振荡与认知的特异性效应。结果显示仅后部10Hz刺激组的alpha功率显著增加，且该效应由alpha振荡爆发时长的增加而非振幅增大所致；但未观察到行为表现或静息态认知的显著变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究表明后部10Hz tACS可普遍增强alpha（7-15Hz）振荡，但该效应是否具有刺激频率和位置的特异性、以及其认知后果仍不清楚。作者旨在厘清tACS对神经振荡和认知的效应是否特异地依赖后部刺激与alpha频率范围。

Method: 采用2×2被试间设计（刺激位置：后部或额顶叶；刺激频率：10Hz或6Hz），另设无刺激对照组，共158名健康被试随机分配。所有被试在持续注意任务期间接受tACS，并于刺激前后进行脑电记录与阿姆斯特丹静息状态问卷评估。使用EEG分析alpha功率及alpha振荡爆发（burst）的时长与幅度指标。

Result: 与对照组相比，仅后部10Hz刺激组的alpha功率显著增加，并且该增加源于alpha振荡爆发的持续时间延长而非幅度增大。在行为表现（持续注意任务）和静息态认知方面，各活动组与对照组之间均无显著差异。

Conclusion: 本研究表明tACS以电极位置和频率特异性的方式延长alpha振荡爆发的时长，但这种调控并未伴随刺激期间持续注意表现或刺激后静息态认知的显著变化。

Abstract: Transcranial alternating current stimulation (tACS) can experimentally manipulate neural oscillations in humans non-invasively. Previous studies consistently suggest that 10 Hz tACS in posterior regions leads to a general increase in alpha (7-15 Hz) oscillations. However, the specificity of this effect regarding stimulation frequency and location as well as its consequences on cognition are still unclear. We therefore aimed to investigate whether the putative effects of tACS on neural oscillations and cognition are specific to posterior stimulation in the alpha frequency range. We tested these effects using a 2 x 2 design (posterior or fronto-parietal stimulation, at 10 Hz or 6 Hz) plus a no-stimulation control group, during a sustained attention task.158 healthy participants were randomly allocated to control group (no stimulation) or one of the four active groups. All participants underwent an Electroencephalography recording and completed the Amsterdam Resting State Questionnaire before and after stimulation. Our results show that, relative to the control group, alpha power increases significantly after stimulation for the group receiving posterior 10 Hz stimulation and that this was explained by an increase in alpha oscillatory bursts duration rather than their amplitude. On the other hand, no significant differences were found between active and controls groups in behavioural performance or resting state cognition. Together, our findings demonstrate that tACS increases the duration of alpha oscillatory bursts in a montage- and frequency-specific manner and that this modulation is not accompanied by significant changes in sustained attention during stimulation or resting state cognition after stimulation.

</details>


### [93] [Universality Meets Cultural Nuances: Affective Responses to Rhythmic and Harmonic Complexity Across Cultures](https://www.biorxiv.org/content/10.64898/2026.08.31.748217v1)
*Klarlund, M.,Hua, S.,Matthews, T.,Stupacher, J.,Brattico, E.,Du, Y.,Vuust, P.*

Main category: bioRxiv.Neuroscience

TL;DR: 本研究通过两个在线实验，比较中国与丹麦听众对音乐节奏与和声复杂度的愉悦感及律动欲（groove）反应。结果显示两组对节奏复杂度均呈倒U型反应（支持预测编码理论的普适性），但丹麦人反应曲线更陡峭（区分度更高），而和声复杂度上两组均未出现倒U型模式，中国人也未表现出更高的和声敏感度。结论支持节奏复杂度的预测反应具有跨文化普遍性，同时文化暴露对音乐情感体验起调节作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前groove与节奏、和声复杂度之间的倒U型关联主要在西方听众中证实，缺乏跨文化普遍性证据。鉴于中国听众对音高与和声敏感度的既有报道，作者旨在检验文化背景是否调节对节奏与和声复杂度的愉悦反应。

Method: 开展两项在线实验：实验1同时操控节奏与和声复杂度，让中国与丹麦被试对音乐片段进行"愉悦感"和"想动感"评分；实验2聚焦节奏复杂度，使用生态效度更高的刺激与更细的梯度分级，进一步比较两组反应。

Result: 实验1中两组对节奏复杂度均呈倒U型反应，但丹麦被试曲线更陡，情感区分更强；和声复杂度未呈现倒U型模式，中国人也未表现出增强的和声敏感度。实验2再次显示丹麦人对节奏变化更敏感（倒U曲线曲率更大），但两组峰值反应无显著差异。

Conclusion: 研究支持对节奏复杂度的预测性（倒U型）反应具有跨文化普遍性，同时强调文化暴露在塑造音乐情感体验中起调节作用，并非和声敏感度的简单文化差异所能一概而论。

Abstract: The pleasurable urge to move to music - known as groove - has been linked to rhythmic and harmonic complexity via an inverted-U relationship, consistent with predictive coding theory of music. This link has been demonstrated mainly in Western listeners, leaving its cultural generality unclear. Given reports of heightened pitch and harmony sensitivity in Chinese listeners, we predicted stronger responses to harmonic complexity and weaker responses to rhythmic complexity compared with Western listeners. We conducted two online experiments measuring "pleasure" and "wanting to move" ratings for musical excerpts varying in rhythmic and harmonic complexity, comparing Chinese and Danish participants as representatives of Eastern and Western traditions. In Experiment 1, which manipulated both dimensions, both groups showed inverted-U responses to rhythmic complexity, but Danish participants exhibited a sharper curve, indicating greater affective differentiation. No inverted-U pattern emerged for harmonic complexity, and Chinese participants did not show enhanced harmonic sensitivity. Experiment 2 focused on rhythmic complexity using ecologically valid stimuli with finer gradations. Again, Danes showed greater sensitivity to rhythmic variation, reflected in a sharper curvature of their inverted U responses, though peak responses did not differ between groups. Our findings support the universality of predictive responses to rhythmic complexity, while highlighting the modulatory role of cultural exposure in shaping musical affective experiences.

</details>


### [94] [Frontal stimulation reshapes SCAN connectivity and improves motor function in Parkinson's disease](https://www.biorxiv.org/content/10.64898/2026.08.31.748359v1)
*Panda, R.,Brissenden, J. A.,Scerbak, T.,Proctor, E.,Li, Y.,Vesia, M.,Albin, R.,Lee, T.*

Main category: bioRxiv.Neuroscience

TL;DR: 本研究通过结合经颅磁刺激（TMS）与功能磁共振成像（fMRI），证实背外侧前额叶皮层（DLPFC）对帕金森病患者的运动功能具有因果性调节作用。兴奋性刺激（iTBS）施加于右DLPFC可改善精细力追踪任务的运动表现，尤其是在高认知负荷（双任务）条件下效果更显著，同时伴随刺激部位与初级运动皮层及体感-认知动作网络（SCAN）之间功能连接的降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有证据表明帕金森病中的一些典型运动缺陷（如冻结步态）与额叶皮层处理异常相关，但人类研究仅能提供认知障碍与运动/步态缺陷之间的相关性证据，缺乏因果性证据。研究者希望验证额叶认知控制系统是否对帕金森病的运动功能产生因果影响。

Method: 采用被试内实验设计：帕金森病患者在执行精密力追踪任务（与步态障碍相关的任务）的同时进行fMRI扫描，其中一半试验同时执行2-back工作记忆任务以增加认知负荷。随后进行三次独立的、顺序平衡的TMS-fMRI结合会话，在任务前分别对右DLPFC施加兴奋性刺激（iTBS）、抑制性刺激（cTBS），并将第三会话刺激施加到对照脑区（非认知和运动相关区域）。

Result: 兴奋性刺激DLPFC改善了精密力追踪任务的运动表现（追踪误差减少、运动执行更平滑），该效应在认知负荷高的双任务条件下尤为显著。改善的运动表现与刺激部位和初级运动皮层之间以及体感-认知动作网络（SCAN）内部的功能连接降低相一致。

Conclusion: 额叶认知控制系统在帕金森病的运动调节中发挥因果作用，提示针对这些系统的非侵入性神经调控干预可能有助于增强帕金森病患者的运动功能。

Abstract: Parkinson's disease is a neurodegenerative disorder with characteristic motor deficits of bradykinesia and impaired coordination, and non-motor deficits affecting cognitive abilities such as attention and executive functions. Recent evidence suggests that some canonical motor deficits in Parkinson's disease (e.g., freezing of gait) are associated with abnormalities in frontal cortical processing typically thought to underlie executive functions such as cognitive control. The evidence from human research linking frontal cognitive disruption and motor deficits in people with Parkinson's disease is correlational. Individuals with cognitive impairments often also display gait and balance deficits, but causal evidence remains lacking. We combined non-invasive brain stimulation with functional MRI (fMRI) to ascertain whether dorsolateral prefrontal cortex (DLPFC) exerts a causal influence modulating motor function and to test the hypothesis that an excitatory stimulation protocol applied to a cognitive control network can improve motor performance in Parkinson disease. Participants with Parkinson's disease performed a precision force-tracking task shown to track with gait impairment while undergoing functional neuroimaging scans. On half of trials, participants were required to simultaneously perform a cognitively demanding 2-back working memory task to tax attentional systems. Following the initial baseline scan, participants returned for three separate counterbalanced sessions that combined transcranial magnetic stimulation (TMS) in the form of theta burst stimulation (TBS) with fMRI. Just prior to task performance, participants received either excitatory (intermittent TBS) or inhibitory (continuous TBS) stimulation protocols over right DLPFC. In the third session, participants received stimulation to a control site outside the cognitive and motor areas implicated in our task. Our within-subjects experimental design allowed us to examine the effects of stimulation on motor behavior, the influence of cognitive load on motor behavior, and the neural systems supporting cognitive control and motor execution. Consistent with our hypothesis, excitatory stimulation of DLPFC improved motor performance in a precision force-tracking task as indexed by reduced tracking error and smoother movement execution. These effects emerged preferentially under dual-task conditions when cognitive demands were high. Our results show that this improved motor performance coincides with reductions in functional connectivity not only between the stimulation site and primary motor cortex, but also within the recently described somato-cognitive action network (SCAN) thought to support action planning and whole-body movement coordination. We conclude that frontal cognitive control systems play a causal role in modulating movement in Parkinson's disease and suggest that targeting these systems with non-invasive interventions may enhance motor function.

</details>


### [95] [Predictive learning with local plasticity in excitatory-inhibitory networks](https://www.biorxiv.org/content/10.64898/2026.08.31.748357v1)
*Reis Aguiar, H.,Hennig, M. H.*

Main category: bioRxiv.Neuroscience

TL;DR: 本文证明了一个具有纯局部可塑性的循环兴奋-抑制（E-I）电路可以在不显式表示预测误差的情况下执行预测推理和表征学习，并通过一致流形条件建立了预测编码与局部电路可塑性之间的直接分析联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测编码是理解皮层计算的有力规范框架，但如何在具有局部可塑性的生物合理网络中实现预测推理和表征学习仍是一个悬而未决的问题，尤其是不清楚是否需要显式的误差表示。

Method: 作者建立了一个循环E-I电路模型，通过闭式推导获得一致条件（consistency condition），推导出一种在高斯先验下精确维持权重在一致流形上的塑性规则。利用这一规则在非高斯先验下学习稀疏因子化特征；通过BCM样规则（含活动依赖阈值）近似该塑性规则；并引入循环兴奋以获取时空特征，研究了时间连续学习下的吸引子结构与噪声驱动的重放。

Result: 该方法可从不含显式预测误差表示的局部可塑性中实现预测推理；能学习自然图像中的稀疏边缘检测器；BCM样规则优于其他Hebbian样规则（后者偏离一致流形导致精度下降）；加入循环兴奋后网络获得方向选择性、序列补全能力、低维吸引子样结构和噪声驱动的重放现象。

Conclusion: 这些结果将预测编码与局部电路可塑性联系起来，表明预测推理不需要显式的预测误差表示，并为興奮性突触中的BCM样塑性提供了一种规范性（normative）解释。

Abstract: Predictive coding is a powerful normative framework for understanding cortical computation, but it is still an open question how biologically plausible networks with local plasticity support predictive inference and representation learning. In this work we show that a recurrent excitatory-inhibitory circuit with purely local plasticity can perform predictive inference without explicit error representations. We establish a direct analytic link that shows that learning in these circuits requires the weights to remain on a consistency manifold where recurrent inhibition matches the inhibition required by the predictive coding objective. Using a closed-form derivation of the consistency condition, we derive a plasticity rule that maintains it exactly under a Gaussian prior. Under a non-Gaussian prior, we find the rule supports learning sparse, factorized features, such as edge detectors from natural images. We show empirically that a BCM-like rule with an activity-dependent threshold approximates this well, while other Hebbian-like rules tend to learn less accurate solutions because they keep the weights too far from this manifold. With recurrent excitation the networks acquire spatiotemporal features like direction selectivity, and the ability to complete partially observed sequences. Time-continuous learning then leads to the development of low-dimensional attractor-like structures and noise-driven replay. Overall, these results link predictive coding to local circuit plasticity, show it does not require an explicit prediction error representation, and suggest a normative role for BCM-like plasticity in excitatory synapses.

</details>


### [96] [A single-nucleus regulatory atlas links transposable elements to adult hippocampal neurogenesis](https://www.biorxiv.org/content/10.64898/2026.08.31.748376v1)
*Cakir, U.,Mantovani, F.,Abadi, S. Y.,Butt, U. J.,Bansal, V.,Kawaguchi, R.,Geschwind, D.,Nave, K.-A.,Ehrenreich, H.,Singh, M.*

Main category: bioRxiv.Neuroscience

TL;DR: 本研究利用snATAC-seq（约3.5万个核）和snRNA-seq（约12万个核）对rhEPO处理与安慰剂处理的成年小鼠海马进行整合分析，解析了外源刺激诱导成年海马神经发生时的谱系特异性染色质调控变化，发现大量差异性可及区域集中在锥体神经元新生细胞中，并揭示转座子（TE）来源序列是刺激应答性神经发生调控网络的关键候选组件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 成年海马神经发生可被外界刺激增强或触发，但伴随刺激诱导的神经发生反应所发生的谱系特异性调控变化此前尚未被阐明。作者希望在一个定义明确的刺激（rhEPO使新生锥体神经元富集约2倍）模型下，从单核染色质可及性层面揭示神经发生的谱系特异性调控机制。

Method: 使用重组人促红细胞生成素（rhEPO）作为刺激因子处理成年小鼠海马，对处理组与安慰剂组进行snATAC-seq分析约35,000个核，并与匹配的snRNA-seq（约120,000个核）进行整合。通过35个可及性定义的聚类解析出11个主要谱系，鉴定39,404个差异性可及区域（DARs），并分析候选顺式调控元件（cCREs）与转座子（TEs）的重叠关系及神经发生转录因子的结合富集。

Result: 35个聚类解析出11种主要细胞谱系。39,404个DARs中超过一半分布于锥体神经元，而齿状回颗粒神经元和中间神经元以可及性降低为主；在锥体谱系内，染色质重塑主要发生在新生而非成熟神经元中。基线时约20%的海马cCREs与TEs重叠，在刺激后获得可及性的区域中该比例上升至约50%，多个TE家族被神经发生转录因子显著结合，其邻近基因多具有神经元分化和突触形成的已知功能。

Conclusion: 该研究将TE来源序列定义为刺激应答性神经发生调控网络的候选组成部件，并为成年海马提供了细胞类型特异性的单核分辨率染色质资源，为理解外源刺激如何驱动神经发生的表观遗传调控奠定了基础。

Abstract: Adult hippocampal neurogenesis can be enhanced or even triggered by extrinsic stimuli, but the lineage-specific regulatory changes that accompany a stimulated neurogenic response remain undefined. We used recombinant human erythropoietin (rhEPO), a defined influencing factor that enriches newly formed pyramidal neurons by ~2 fold in this paradigm, to profile ~35,000 nuclei by single-nucleus ATAC sequencing (snATAC-seq) from rhEPO- and placebo-treated adult mouse hippocampi and to integrate them with matched single-nucleus RNA sequencing (snRNA-seq) of ~120,000 nuclei. Thirty-five accessibility-defined clusters resolved 11 major lineages. Of 39,404 differentially accessible regions (DARs), more than half arose in pyramidal neurons, whereas dentate gyrus granule neurons and interneurons showed predominantly reduced accessibility; within the pyramidal lineage, remodelling was predominant in newly-formed rather than mature neurons. About 20% of hippocampal candidate cis-regulatory elements (cCREs) overlapped transposable elements (TEs) at baseline, rising to about 50% among regions that gained accessibility upon stimulation, and multiple families were significantly bound above the expected threshold by neurogenic transcription factors, whereby the many neighbouring genes have established roles for neuronal differentiation and synapse formation. These data define TE-derived sequences as candidate components of stimulus-responsive neurogenic regulatory networks and provide a cell-type-specific chromatin resource for the adult hippocampus at single-nucleus resolution.

</details>


### [97] [Heart rate deceleration reflects expectation violation during reaching](https://www.biorxiv.org/content/10.64898/2026.09.01.748501v1)
*Wu, W. P.,Nozaki, D.,Hayashi, T.*

Main category: bioRxiv.Neuroscience

TL;DR: 本研究通过机器人操纵杆的手臂到达运动范式，在人类被试中解离了运动结果与预期违背对心率（HR）减速反应的贡献，发现HR减速与运动预期的违背关联更密切，而非运动错误本身，提示HR可作为运动中预期相关加工的生理标记。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往研究将运动错误后的心率减速解释为对不良行为结果的自主神经反应，但运动错误不仅代表不良结果，还可能违背预期。研究者希望解离这两种因素对心率反应的影响，以更精确理解心率减速的神经生理意义。

Method: 采用机器人操纵杆的到达运动范式，结合视动扰动诱导运动错误；通过情境线索预测即将发生的运动结果以操纵预期；改变目标宽度以区分任务错误与感觉预测错误，并记录分析HR反应。

Result: 确认视动扰动诱导的运动错误后出现HR减速；当情境线索可预测结果时，HR减速出现在运动准备阶段而非运动错误之后；HR减速与意外运动结果相关而非运动结果的负效价；通过改变目标宽度发现HR减速更紧密关联任务错误而非感觉预测错误。

Conclusion: 运动过程中HR反应更紧密关联运动预期的违背而非运动错误本身，为感觉运动加工中的自主神经反应提供了新视角，提示HR可作为运动过程中预期相关加工的生理标记。

Abstract: Heart rate (HR) deceleration occurs following movement error and has been interpreted as an autonomic response to adverse behavioral outcomes. However, movement errors not only represent adverse movement outcomes but may also violate expectations. The present study employed a reaching movement paradigm using a robotic manipulandum in male and female human participants to dissociate the contributions of movement outcome and expectation violation to HR responses. We confirmed that HR deceleration occurred following visuomotor perturbations that induce movement errors, consistent with previous studies. However, when contextual cues predicted upcoming movement outcomes, HR deceleration emerged during movement preparation rather than following movement error, suggesting the involvement of expectation in HR responses. Moreover, across varying levels of expectation for different movement outcomes, HR deceleration was associated with unexpected movement outcomes rather than with the negative valence of movement outcomes. By altering target width to permit successful movements despite visuomotor rotations, we further found that HR deceleration was more strongly associated with task error than with sensory prediction error. Together, these findings indicate that HR responses during movement are more closely associated with violations of movement expectation than with movement error itself. These findings provide a new perspective on autonomic responses during sensorimotor processing and suggest that HR may serve as a physiological marker of expectation-related processing during movement.

</details>


### [98] [Retrieval uncertainty drives rapid consolidation of confabulated memories in retrosplenial cortex](https://www.biorxiv.org/content/10.64898/2026.09.01.748171v1)
*Singh, A.,Das, N.,Ahuja, P.,Banerjee, A.,Kalvae, S.,sane, A.,Kundu, S.,Rajesh, S.,Saha, T.,Jayaprakash, B.*

Main category: bioRxiv.Neuroscience

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了在无直接强化条件下，不确定性记忆检索是否能引发对未经历事件的高阶联想（HOA）记忆的虚构，发现HOA的习得依赖背侧CA1小白蛋白中间神经元，而检索依赖压后皮层（RSc），并开发了LaBCAM方法绘制脑wide功能连接图谱。HOA在阿尔茨海默病模型及老年动物中早期即出现异常。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于先前奖赏或惩罚记忆在无直接强化下形成联想是一种进化上至关重要的生存特性，但关于高阶联想是否会导致对未真实发生事件产生新的虚构记忆尚不清楚。研究者希望探究旧记忆表征检索过程中的不确定性是否会触发此类虚构（confabulation）。

Method: 采用化学遗传学（chemogenetics）、体内成像（in vivo imaging）和行为学实验相结合。开发了自动化的全脑大尺度关联活动映射方法LaBCAM（Large-scale Brainwide Correlated Activity Mapping）以识别不同脑区间的功能连接变化。同时进行RSc的树突棘纵向成像，并在AD小鼠模型（APP/PS1）及老年动物中进行验证。

Result: 不确定性记忆感知不仅可将条件化迁移至从未经历的全新环境，还能被快速巩固。HOA的习得需要完整海马中背侧CA1小白蛋白中间神经元活动，而检索需要RSc。LaBCAM发现仅不确定的远程检索会激活典型恐惧环路，而常规检索不会。RSc的树突棘纵向成像显示HOA习得与快速系统巩固期间存在差异性的棘重组。此外，HOA在AD模型和老年动物中早期即受牵连。

Conclusion: 不确定性在记忆检索过程中可将旧记忆表征推广到未经历情境，产生虚构的高阶联想记忆并快速固化。该过程有明确的神经环路基础，且其异常可能作为AD等认知退行疾病的早期标志。

Abstract: Learning to make associations in the absence of direct reinforcement, based on previous memories of reward or punishment, is an evolutionary trait quintessential for survival. Little is known about if such higher order associations (HOA) can lead to fabrication of new memories for unreal events. We probed if uncertainty during retrieval of old memory representations can trigger such confabulation. Using chemogenetics, in vivo imaging, and behavior we show that uncertainty in memory perception can not only transfer conditioning to hitherto unexperienced novel context but also can get rapidly consolidated. We find while acquisition of HOA require dorsal-CA1 parvalbumin interneuron activity in intact hippocampus, retrieval requires retrosplenial cortex (RSc). We develop an automated Large-scale Brainwide Correlated Activity Mapping (LaBCAM) method to identify functional connectivity changes across different brain regions in such scenarios. LaBCAM finds that only uncertain remote retrieval engages canonical fear circuitry while regular retrieval does not. Longitudinal imaging of dendritic spines in RSc, a region involved in detection and context-dependent conflict resolution, reveals differential reorganization of spines during acquisition and rapid systems consolidation of HOA. Interestingly, we find HOA is implicated early in AD mice model (APP/PS1) and also in aged animals.

</details>


### [99] [Sleep spindle state-dependent motor cortical plasticity induction by EEG-triggered ripple burst TMS](https://www.biorxiv.org/content/10.64898/2026.09.01.748506v1)
*Zhao, Z.,Lu, Y.,Breuer, F.,Bergmann, T. O.,Ziemann, U.*

Main category: bioRxiv.Neuroscience

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了在睡眠中通过实时EEG突发重复经颅磁刺激（rTMS）靶向不同纺锤波状态（纺锤波谷、纺锤波峰、随机相位及无纺锤波期）对皮质脊髓和感觉运动皮质可塑性的影响。结果显示，仅在纺锤波谷施加海马涟漪频率的rTMS可一致地诱导长时程抑制样可塑性变化，表现为静息态EEGβ频段功率、运动诱发电位幅度、P60-LMFA、即刻反应斜率及α频段TMS相关时频响应的下降，以及N45-LMFA的升高。该发现为靶向操控人类睡眠生理以改善记忆巩固等行为过程提供了新途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 睡眠纺锤波对突触可塑性和记忆巩固至关重要，而海马涟漪（hippocampal ripples）天然嵌套于纺锤波谷中，反映记忆痕迹的重放并促进记忆巩固。作者推测纺锤波谷是诱导可塑性的关键状态，因此希望验证在不同纺锤波状态下施加rTMS能否产生状态依赖性的皮质可塑性改变，从而为靶向操控睡眠生理提供依据。

Method: 纳入14名健康参与者，每人经历4个实验夜，分别接受四种条件的rTMS干预：纺锤波谷期、纺锤波峰期、纺锤波随机相位期或无纺锤波期，刺激频率采用海马涟漪频率。可塑性指标包括静息态EEG（rsEEG）功率、运动诱发电位（MEP）幅度、N45与P60成分的局部平均场幅度（LMFA）、即刻反应斜率（IRS）及TMS相关时频响应（TFR），于干预结束后10分钟和30分钟（清醒后）测定并与睡前基线比较。

Result: 仅在纺锤波谷期施加rTMS可观察到一致且显著的塑性变化：rsEEGβ频段功率降低、MEP幅度下降、P60-LMFA降低、IRS下降、α频段TFR降低，同时N45-LMFA升高。其余三种纺锤波状态相关的rTMS干预均未产生一致的塑性改变。

Conclusion: 以海马涟漪频率rTMS靶向纺锤波谷，可在广泛的皮质脊髓和感觉运动皮质兴奋性指标上一致地诱导长时程抑制样变化，提示纺锤波谷是睡眠中诱导可塑性的关键窗口。该发现为靶向操控人类睡眠生理、进而改善记忆巩固等特定行为过程提供了有前景的新途径。

Abstract: Background: Sleep spindles are fundamental for plasticity and memory consolidation. Here we sought to target different spindle states in sleeping healthy participants with real-time EEG-burst repetitive transcranial magnetic stimulation (rTMS) at hippocampal ripple frequency, and test the spindle state-dependent induction of corticospinal and sensorimotor cortical plasticity. We hypothesized that the spindle-trough is a particularly critical state for plasticity induction because hippocampal ripples are naturally nested in the spindle-trough, reflecting replay of memory traces and facilitating memory consolidation. Methods: Fourteen participants underwent four experimental nights, in which rTMS was applied either at the spindle-trough, spindle-peak, spindle random phase or during spindle-free epochs. Readouts of plasticity were changes in resting-state EEG (rsEEG) power, motor evoked potential (MEP) amplitude, local mean field amplitude (LMFA, for the N45 and P60 potential components), immediate response slope (IRS) and TMS-related time frequency response (TFR), tested 10 and 30 min after the end of the rTMS interventions upon awakening, and compared to pre-sleep baseline. Results: Spindle-trough rTMS resulted in pre- to post-sleep decreases of rsEEG beta-band power, MEP amplitude, P60-LMFA, IRS and TFR in the alpha-band, and an increase in the N45-LMFA. None of the other spindle state-dependent rTMS interventions resulted in consistent plastic changes. Conclusions: Targeting the spindle-trough with ripple-burst rTMS stands out in consistently leading to long-term depression-like changes across a broad array of corticospinal and sensorimotor cortical excitability readouts. This opens the intriguing opportunity of targeted manipulation of human sleep physiology for improving specific behavioral processes, such as memory consolidation.

</details>


### [100] [A computational model of the two dentate gyrus blades](https://www.biorxiv.org/content/10.64898/2026.09.01.748300v1)
*Studenyak, V.,Jost, J.,Doeller, C. F.,Bicanski, A.*

Main category: bioRxiv.Neuroscience

TL;DR: 本文提出首个计算模型来刻画齿状回（DG）两个叶片的功能差异：上锥体层DG（DGSUP）通过基于范例的k-WTA架构支持模式分离和情景特异表征，而下锥体层DG（DGINF）通过渐进异突触可塑性支持跨情景的模式整合。模型在MNIST和神经可塑的内嗅皮层输入两个数据集上测试，两个叶片分别形成重映射或稳定编码的位置野，并通过神经发生启发的周转和招募机制容纳新输入。该双流处理架构通过预测误差驱动记忆存储与遗忘，将DG功能从模式分离扩展到空间认知地图构建与情景记忆痕迹的保留。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上齿状回被视为专注于模式分离的脑区，但实验证据表明其功能远超于此，包括物体与空间/事件的精确绑定以及跨情景信息整合。更重要的是，近年研究表明DG的两个叶片（上锥体层和下锥体层）分别偏向模式分离与模式整合，但此前缺乏计算模型来解释这一功能分化。本文旨在填补这一空白，建立第一个能够刻画DG双叶片功能差异的计算模型。

Method: 作者提出双架构模型：1）DGSUP采用基于范例的k-WTA（k-胜者全取）稀疏编码架构，支持模式分离并产生情景特异表征；2）DGINF采用渐进式异突触可塑性架构，逐步整合跨情景的模式。两种编码机制分别在MNIST和神经可塑的内嗅皮层输入数据集上测试。模型还引入神经发生启发的神经元周转与招募机制来处理新颖输入（如新数字类别和新空间情景）。通过比较两个处理流（即时经验vs.跨情景的广义预期），模型计算预测误差，用于驱动差预测记忆的存储和好预测记忆的遗忘。

Result: 在内嗅皮层输入下，两个叶片分别形成重映射（remap）或保持稳定编码的位置野，与实验结果一致。模型能有效处理新颖输入（新数字类别、新空间情景），并通过双流比较产生的预测误差驱动记忆选择性的存储与遗忘。两种编码机制在两类数据集上均表现出一定领域的通用性。结果表明该模型能够同时支持空间认知地图的迭代构建（编码位置依赖的预期）和个体情景记忆痕迹的保留。

Conclusion: 作者提出并验证了首个区分DG双叶片功能的计算模型，采用生物学上合理的可塑性机制（k-WTA稀疏编码、渐进异突触可塑性、神经发生启发的周转），将DG的计算功能从单一的模式分离扩展到跨情景整合、预测误差计算及空间认知地图的动态构建，显著拓宽了对DG功能范围的理解。

Abstract: The Dentate Gyrus (DG) is a key part of the hippocampus, and damage to the DG produces a wide range of pathologies, including overgeneralization of contexts, affective dysregulation (Anacker et al., 2018), and epileptogenic effects (Sloviter, 1994). The canonical model of the DG focuses on pattern separation for subsequent memory storage in the hippocampal subfield CA3. Experimental results challenge the singular focus on pattern separation and extend the function of the DG to the precise binding of objects and events to space, and the integration of information across episodes. Recent studies suggest that pattern separation and integration preferentially rely on distinct DG blades, with the suprapyramidal and infrapyramidal blades biased toward separation and integration, respectively. Here, we propose the first computational model that accounts for this distinction: an exemplar-based k-WTA architecture in the suprapyramidal DG (DGSUP) supports pattern separation and episode-specific representations, whereas an architecture with gradual heterosynaptic plasticity in the infrapyramidal DG (DGINF) supports integration of patterns across episodes. Both coding regimes are tested with two datasets: MNIST and neurally plausible entorhinal cortex inputs, thus suggesting some domain generality. Using the entorhinal cortex inputs, the two blades form place fields that either remap or maintain a stable code, consistent with experimental results. Novel inputs, including novel digit classes and novel spatial episodes, are incorporated through a neurogenesis-inspired turnover and recruitment mechanism. The two processing streams allow for a comparison of ongoing experience with the generalized expectations formed through integration across episodes. This yields prediction errors that can drive the storage of poorly predicted memories and the forgetting of well-predicted memories. The differential processing across the DG could thus aid in the iterative construction of spatial cognitive maps that encode location-dependent expectations, while at the same time preserving individual episodic memory traces. These functions are accomplished with biologically plausible learning regimes and widen the scope of DG computation beyond its well-established role in pattern separation.

</details>

# cs.NE [[Back]](#toc)

### [101] [Efficient Constant Optimization for Symbolic Regression with GPU-Accelerated Tree-Based Genetic Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03352)
*Hao Mao,Xu Tony Liu,Shuai Lu,Peng Zhao,Wenzheng Jiang,Yuntian Chen*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种GPU驻留的批处理Levenberg--Marquardt求解器，用于符号回归遗传编程中表达式树的常数优化。它利用反向模式自动微分在一次后向扫描中组装每棵树的雅可比矩阵，使每代成本不再依赖每棵树的常数个数，并带有双精度交付保护机制。在NVIDIA A100上可达到5.1×10⁵棵树/秒，在GPU饱和配置下约为运行在64核EPYC 7763上的Operon吞吐量的9.9倍，且保持fp64参考质量；集成到EvoGP后，端到端搜索在18个构造问题中恢复出10个数学模型（原生EvoGP为0个）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于树的遗传编程在符号回归中的常数优化每代成本高昂，现代GPU加速框架往往省略此操作或仅采用轻量形式，限制了搜索解的质量与收敛性。

Method: 提出GPU驻留、批处理式的Levenberg--Marquardt求解器，在结构异构的表达式树上用固定数量的跨种群CUDA启动次数优化常数；通过反向模式自动微分在一次反向扫描中组装单棵树雅可比，使单次迭代主要成本与各树常数数量无关；并引入双精度交付保护以确保返回常数不劣于初值。

Result: 在早代种群上，A100上维持高达5.1×10⁵棵树/秒的处理速度；在GPU饱和基准配置下吞吐量约为64核EPYC 7763上Operon的9.9倍；同时匹配fp64参考精度。集成到EvoGP后，端到端搜索在18个构造问题上恢复10个控制方程，而原生EvoGP为0个。

Conclusion: GPU驻留的批处理LM常数优化可以高效地应用于异构表达式树种群，显著提升符号回归搜索的计算效率与恢复可解释数学模型的成功率，为GPU加速GP框架补充高性价比的常数优化能力提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Constant optimization refines the numerical coefficients of candidate expressions in tree-based genetic programming for symbolic regression. But its per-generation cost has led modern GPU-accelerated frameworks to omit it or restrict it to lightweight forms. We present a GPU-resident, batched Levenberg--Marquardt solver that optimizes constants across a structurally heterogeneous population of expression trees using a fixed number of population-wide CUDA launches per iteration. Reverse-mode automatic differentiation assembles the per-tree Jacobian in one backward sweep, making the dominant per-iteration cost independent of the number of constants per tree, and a double-precision delivery guard guarantees that returned constants are never worse than their initial values. On early-generation populations, the solver sustains up to $5.1{\times}10^{5}$ trees per second on an NVIDIA A100; at a GPU-saturated benchmark configuration it delivers roughly $9.9{\times}$ the throughput of Operon running on a 64-core EPYC 7763, while matching fp64-reference quality. Integrated in-process into EvoGP, the solver enables end-to-end search to recover governing equations on $10$ of $18$ constructed problems versus 0 for stock EvoGP. Our code is at https://github.com/TensorConv/CuSR.

</details>


### [102] [Genetic Algorithms for Tractable Bayesian Network Fusion via Pre-Fusion Edge Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03724)
*Pablo Torrijos,José A. Gámez,José M. Puerta,Juan A. Aledo*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于共识的贝叶斯网络融合框架，通过遗传算法优先保留输入网络的共享结构并强制执行树宽约束，在依赖保留与计算可扩展性之间取得平衡。实验表明该方法优于改编的现有方法和贪婪基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝叶斯网络融合面临两难：无限制融合保留所有依赖但导致高树宽、影响推理可扩展性；限制融合虽控制树宽却可能过拟合输入噪声并遗漏网络间差异结构。需要一个能优先权衡输入网络共识结构并约束树宽的融合方案。

Method: 提出共识框架，优先保留输入网络间的共享结构并强制执行树宽约束。设计带高级初始化、专门算子和定制适应度函数的遗传算法；同时将现有方法改编到该问题，并实现贪婪基线用于基准测试和进一步优化。

Result: 在合成和真实贝叶斯网络上的实验表明，所提出的遗传算法在融合性能上优于改编的现有方法和贪婪基线。

Conclusion: 基于共识的遗传算法框架能有效解决贝叶斯网络融合中依赖保留与树宽可控之间的权衡问题，为多网络融合提供更优的解决方案。

Abstract: Bayesian Network (BN) fusion combines multiple input networks into a single structure, balancing dependency preservation with computational tractability. While unrestricted fusion retains all dependencies, it often results in overly complex networks with high treewidth, which affects inference scalability. Limited fusion mitigates this by pruning edges to control treewidth but risks overfitting to input-specific noise and omitting dependencies from the original BNs. This paper introduces a consensus framework that prioritizes shared structures among input networks while enforcing treewidth constraints, ensuring a good consensus. We propose genetic algorithms with advanced initialization, specialized operators, and a tailored fitness function. Additionally, we adapt existing methods to this problem and implement greedy baselines for benchmarking and further optimization. Experiments on synthetic and real-world BNs show the superiority of the proposed genetic algorithms over the adapted methods and greedy baselines.

</details>
